================================================================================
AI Code Editor — Phase 2: Real Local File System Access
================================================================================

STATUS: complete. `cd client && npm run dev`, then open http://localhost:5173
        in Chrome / Edge / Brave and click "Open Folder".

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
WHAT CHANGED (high level)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Phase 1 read from a hard-coded dummy tree. This phase replaces that with the
browser's File System Access API, so clicking "Open Folder" gives the editor
real read/write access to a folder on the user's laptop.

The dummy tree was NOT deleted. It now acts as an offline fallback: it is shown
until a real folder is opened, and the explorer displays a notice telling the
user to click "Open Folder". Once a folder is opened, the dummy tree is
discarded permanently for that session.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEW FILES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

1. client/src/services/fileSystemAccess.js
   The only module that touches the File System Access API.

   HANDLE REGISTRY
   FileSystemHandle objects are not serializable, so they are never put in
   Redux. They live in a module-level Map:

       registry: id -> { handle, parentId, name, type }

   Redux only ever stores plain data: { id, name, type, children, isOpen }.

   - isFileSystemAccessSupported()  -> true when 'showDirectoryPicker' in window
   - getHandleById(id)              -> the FileSystemHandle for a node id
   - getRegistryEntry(id)           -> { handle, parentId, name, type } or null
   - getParentHandle(id)            -> parent directory handle (null for root)
   - registerHandle(handle, meta)   -> stores a handle under a new uuid and
                                       returns the plain Redux node. Passing an
                                       existing `id` rebinds that slot, which is
                                       how a rename keeps its open tab.
   - forgetHandles(ids)             -> drops stale registry entries

   PERMISSIONS
   - requirePermission(handle, mode = 'readwrite')
       queryPermission() first, then requestPermission() if not granted, and
       throws if the user declines. Called before every write operation.
       Must run from a user gesture for the browser to show its prompt.

   READING
   - openFolder()
       window.showDirectoryPicker({ id: 'code-matrix' }), stores and returns the
       root DirectoryHandle. Called with no arguments before any other await so
       the picker keeps its user activation.
   - readDirectoryTree(directoryHandle)
       Recursively walks a directory with its async iterator
       (for await (const [name, handle] of directoryHandle.entries())), returning
       a nested node { id: crypto.randomUUID(), name, type, isOpen, children }.
       Resets the registry. The root is returned expanded. Entries are sorted
       folders-first, then alphabetically by name.
   - reindexDirectory(directoryHandle, previousNode)
       Re-reads a directory that was just renamed, reusing the ids of entries
       that still exist so open tabs and unsaved buffers survive the rename.
   - readFileContent(fileHandle)
       fileHandle.getFile(), then .text().

   WRITING
   - writeFileContent(fileHandle, content)
       createWritable(), write(content), close() (close runs in a finally block
       so a failed write cannot leave the stream open).
   - createNewFile(parentDirectoryHandle, fileName)
       parentDirectoryHandle.getFileHandle(fileName, { create: true })
   - createNewFolder(parentDirectoryHandle, folderName)
       parentDirectoryHandle.getDirectoryHandle(folderName, { create: true })
   - deleteEntry(parentDirectoryHandle, name, type)
       parentDirectoryHandle.removeEntry(name, { recursive: type === 'folder' })
       (`recursive` is only passed for folders, matching the API's contract.)
   - renameEntry(parentDirectoryHandle, oldName, newName, type)
       The API has no atomic rename, so this is copy + delete:
         * file   -> read the source File blob, write it to the new entry, then
                     remove the old entry. Writing the blob (not text) keeps
                     binary files byte-for-byte intact.
         * folder -> copyDirectory() recurses through every entry, then removes
                     the original directory with { recursive: true }.
       Returns the handle of the new entry.

   SCAN SCOPE
   - SKIP_DIRECTORIES = new Set(['node_modules', '.git'])
     These are skipped at every level while walking. Without this, opening a
     typical JS project freezes the tab and produces an unusable tree. Delete or
     extend this constant to change the behaviour.

2. client/src/hooks/useOpenFolder.js
   - openLocalFolder(): support check -> openFolder() -> readDirectoryTree() ->
     dispatch(loadWorkspace) -> success toast. A cancelled picker (AbortError) is
     ignored silently; anything else becomes an error toast.
   - returns { openLocalFolder, isOpening, isSupported } so the button can show a
     spinner and disable itself while a large folder is being walked.
   - exports UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE:
     "Your browser doesn't support local folder access. Please use Chrome, Edge,
      or Brave."

3. client/src/hooks/useSaveActiveFile.js
   Writes the active editor buffer back to disk. Shared by the Topbar Save button
   and Ctrl/Cmd+S so both behave identically.
   - no file open            -> info toast
   - demo workspace          -> info toast explaining to open a folder first
   - handle missing          -> error toast ("Lost access to <file>...")
   - writeFileContent fails  -> error toast with the underlying message
   - success                 -> markSaved() + "Saved <name>" toast

4. client/src/components/Toast/Toast.jsx + Toast.css
   Renders ui.toast in the top-right, with auto-dismiss timers per type
   (success 2.6s, info 3.6s, error 6s) and a manual close button.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
MODIFIED FILES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

client/src/features/fileSystemSlice.js
  - state gained `workspace: { name, source }` where source is 'demo' | 'local'.
  - `tree` holds plain nodes only; handle creation moved to the service.
  - loadWorkspace({ tree, name })  replaces the tree, clears openTabs and
    activeFileId, and flips workspace.source to 'local'.
  - addNode({ parentId, node })    now takes a fully built node instead of
    generating one with nanoid, because the service owns id creation.
  - replaceSubtree({ id, name, children, removedFileIds })  used after a folder
    rename: the folder keeps its id, its children are swapped for the re-read
    subtree, and any file ids that vanished are pruned from openTabs.
  - pruneTabs() helper shared by deleteNode and replaceSubtree.
  - renameNode / toggleFolder / setFolderOpen / collapseAll / addTab / closeTab
    unchanged in behaviour.

client/src/features/editorSlice.js
  - contentByFileId starts seeded from the dummy tree, so demo files still open
    instantly; loadWorkspace clears it.
  - NEW setContent({ fileId, content }): buffers content read from disk without
    flagging the file as unsaved.
  - updateContent() still marks a file unsaved.
  - extraReducers now also prune content on replaceSubtree and reset everything
    on loadWorkspace.

client/src/features/uiSlice.js
  - added `toast` state plus showToast (with a nanoid via prepare) and hideToast.

client/src/components/Topbar/Topbar.jsx + Topbar.css
  - NEW "Open Folder" button (FolderOpen icon) with a loading spinner.
  - NEW workspace chip next to the logo showing the demo workspace name or the
    real folder name (accent-coloured when a local folder is open).
  - Save button now calls useSaveActiveFile() instead of just clearing the
    unsaved flag.

client/src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.jsx
  - Owns all disk I/O for the explorer and passes handlers down to FileTree.
  - startCreating / submitCreate: resolves the target parent from the last
    selected row (selected folder -> itself, selected file -> its parent, nothing
    selected -> workspace root) and calls createNewFile / createNewFolder.
  - handleRename: renameEntry() on disk. A file rename rebinds the handle under
    the same node id, so the open tab and its buffer survive. A folder rename
    re-reads the new folder (reindexDirectory) and dispatches replaceSubtree.
  - handleDelete: deleteEntry() then deleteNode + forgetHandles for the subtree.
  - Demo workspace: all four actions fall back to pure Redux updates.
  - Shows the unsupported-browser banner, and the "No folder opened" notice while
    the demo tree is in use.

client/src/components/Sidebar/FileTree.jsx
  - Now purely presentational for disk operations: calls onRename / onDelete
    passed down from the Sidebar instead of dispatching rename/delete itself.
  - `lockedId` hides the rename/delete actions for the workspace root.
  - Row click still opens a file as a tab in Redux; content is fetched later by
    the editor.

client/src/components/Editor/EditorArea.jsx
  - Lazily loads real content: when a file becomes active and is not already in
    contentByFileId, it looks up the handle and calls readFileContent(), then
    dispatches setContent. Tabs already loaded are never re-read.
  - Renders three distinct states: a loading placeholder ("Opening <file>…"), an
    error panel if the read fails, and Monaco once content is ready.
  - Failure state is keyed by file id, so switching tabs needs no reset effect.
  - Monaco keeps `keepCurrentModel`, so undo history and scroll position survive
    tab switches.

client/src/components/Editor/EmptyState.jsx
  - Workspace-aware: explains "No folder opened. Click Open Folder in the top bar
    to edit real files from your laptop" in demo mode, shows the unsupported
    browser warning when showDirectoryPicker is missing, and names the open local
    folder in the footer.

client/src/hooks/useGlobalShortcuts.js
  - Ctrl/Cmd+S now routes through useSaveActiveFile(), writing to the real file.

client/src/utils/tree.js
  - NEW collectAllIds(node)             every id in a subtree
  - NEW newNodeFileIds(previous, next)  file ids that disappeared between two
                                        versions of a subtree
  - buildContentMap() is still used to seed the demo buffers.

client/src/App.jsx           renders <Toast />.
client/src/index.css         shared .spin animation moved here from Topbar.css.
client/src/components/Editor/Editor.css   styles for .editor-status and the
                                          empty-state banner.
client/src/components/Sidebar/Sidebar.css styles for .sidebar-notice.
client/src/components/Topbar/Topbar.css   styles for .text-btn, .workspace-chip.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
HOW THE PIECES CONNECT NOW
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Topbar
  "Open Folder" -> useOpenFolder -> showDirectoryPicker -> readDirectoryTree ->
  loadWorkspace(tree). Save -> useSaveActiveFile -> writeFileContent(handle).

Sidebar
  Clicking a row -> Redux only (selection + expand/collapse + open tab).
  New / Rename / Delete -> real API calls -> Redux updated with the result.
  Every mutation is "disk first, then Redux", so the tree can never claim a file
  exists when it does not.

Editor
  Opening a tab -> getHandleById -> readFileContent -> setContent (clean buffer).
  Typing -> updateContent (marks unsaved).
  Ctrl+S / Save -> writeFileContent -> markSaved + toast.

Redux
  Holds serializable tree data, tab state and buffers. The handle registry lives
  outside Redux, keyed by the same node ids.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
LIMITATIONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

BROWSER SUPPORT
  Requires a Chromium browser (Chrome, Edge, Brave) on https or localhost.
  Firefox and Safari do not implement showDirectoryPicker. The app detects this
  and shows "Your browser doesn't support local folder access. Please use Chrome,
  Edge, or Brave." in both the explorer and the welcome screen. Everything still
  works in demo mode on those browsers.

SECURE CONTEXT
  crypto.randomUUID() and the File System Access API both need a secure context.
  The Vite dev server runs on http://localhost, which counts as secure, so this
  works out of the box in development. A production build must be served over
  HTTPS.

NO NATIVE RENAME
  The File System Access API has no rename/move operation. renameEntry() copies
  the entry and deletes the original, which means:
    - Renaming a large file or a folder with many files blocks the UI until the
      copy finishes; there is no progress indicator.
    - A rename is not atomic. If the tab is closed mid-copy, both the old and the
      new entry can exist on disk.
    - Metadata (timestamps) is not preserved. File contents are, including
      binary data.

PERMISSIONS ARE PER SESSION
  Write permission is granted per folder and is dropped on every page reload.
  Handles are intentionally kept in memory only and are never persisted to
  IndexedDB, so a refresh always requires picking the folder and granting access
  again. The first write to a folder triggers the browser's permission prompt.

SCAN SCOPE
  node_modules and .git are skipped at every level. Everything else is walked
  eagerly and fully on "Open Folder", so a very large or deeply nested directory
  can still take a while. There is also no file watcher: changes made to the
  folder by another program will not show up until the folder is reopened.

DEMO WORKSPACE
  Save, create, rename and delete in the demo workspace only touch Redux. Nothing
  is written to disk, and the changes are lost on refresh.

BUFFERS AND UNSUPPORTED FILES
  readFileContent() decodes files as UTF-8 text. Opening a binary file (image,
  archive, compiled artefact) will show mojibake; it will not be corrupted unless
  you save it, which would replace the binary with text.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERIFICATION PERFORMED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - oxlint: 0 warnings, 0 errors across 22 files.
  - vite build: succeeds.
  - 36 checks against services/fileSystemAccess.js driven in Chromium against the
    Origin Private File System (same FileSystemHandle API): create, write, read,
    rename file (incl. binary round trip), rename folder recursively, reindex
    id reuse, delete file/folder, registry helpers, sorted tree output, and proof
    that tree nodes carry no handle.
  - 40 end-to-end checks driving the real UI over such a workspace: opening a
    folder, expanding folders, lazy content load, edit -> unsaved dot, save ->
    bytes changed on disk, create file/folder on disk, rename file (tab survives),
    rename folder (contents and tabs survive), delete file/folder, toast
    lifecycle, and zero page errors.
  - 14 checks covering the demo fallback: tree, notice, seeded content, save
    refusal message, and local-only create/rename/delete.
  - Bug found and fixed during testing: after opening a folder the root row was
    collapsed, so the explorer appeared empty. readDirectoryTree() now returns
    the root expanded.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEXT STEPS / IDEAS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - Persist handles in IndexedDB so a refresh can reuse the last folder after a
    single permission re-grant.
  - Add a "Refresh" action that re-runs reindexDirectory on the root (the id
    reuse is already in place for it).
  - Add a file watcher via FileSystemObserver where supported.
  - Warn before overwriting a binary file opened as text.


================================================================================
AI Code Editor — Phase 3: Cloud Workspace + Real Terminal
================================================================================

STATUS: complete. Start the API first (`cd server && node server.js`) and the
        client (`cd client && npm run dev`), open http://localhost:5173 in a
        Chromium browser, then click "Open Folder" to import a project.

The editor no longer edits the laptop folder directly. "Open Folder" now copies
the whole folder up to a server-side workspace, and every later file operation
goes through the workspace API. The only time the laptop is touched again is the
one-off import and the explicit "Sync to Laptop" action.

NOTE ON PRE-EXISTING WORK: the entire server side (Parts 1 and 3) already
existed and was verified working; this entry was primarily wiring the client
(Part 2 and Part 4) to it. No server source file was changed in this entry.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ARCHITECTURE / WHY IT IS SHAPED THIS WAY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Replit free-tier Autoscale has an EPHEMERAL disk: server/workspaces is wiped
whenever the server sleeps or restarts. Consequences baked into the design:
  - MongoDB stores ONLY metadata (Project.model.js: projectId, name, ownerId,
    diskPath, lastAccessedAt). Never file contents.
  - Files live temporarily at server/workspaces/<projectId>/ on disk.
  - The UI warns the user repeatedly and pushes them to export.
  - Every workspace route checks the folder exists and answers 404
    WORKSPACE_MISSING when it does not.

REDUX TREE IDS ARE PATHS
For a cloud project every tree node's `id` is its workspace-relative path (the
root keeps the literal id "root"). This means tab ids, editor buffer keys and API
arguments are all the same string, so no id<->path map is needed. Demo-mode nodes
keep their old random ids.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SERVER (unchanged this entry, documented for completeness)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
server/src/models/Project.model.js       metadata-only mongoose schema
server/src/models/project.repository.js  degradable DB helpers (no-op if no DB)
server/src/utils/workspaceManager.js     all disk I/O + path-traversal guards
server/src/utils/terminalManager.js      node-pty session Map (optional dependency)
server/src/socket/terminalSocket.js      Socket.IO event handlers
server/src/controllers/workspace.controller.js
server/src/routes/workspace.routes.js    mounted at /api/workspace
server/src/middlewares/ensureWorkspace.js  404 WORKSPACE_MISSING guard
server/src/middlewares/validateProjectId.js rejects unsafe project ids
server/server.js                         http.createServer + Socket.IO shared

Deployed packages (server): archiver, socket.io, node-pty (optionalDependency;
terminal degrades to a clear error if the native module cannot load).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
API ROUTES (all under /api)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
GET    /api/health                       health/uptime/db/terminal status
POST   /api/workspace/:projectId/import  { name?, files:[{relativePath,content}] }
                                         -> creates folder + writes files + upserts
                                            Project, returns { fileCount, tree }
                                         (max 5000 files; body limit 50mb)
GET    /api/workspace/:projectId/tree    nested tree; 404 WORKSPACE_MISSING if gone
GET    /api/workspace/:projectId/file?path=...
PUT    /api/workspace/:projectId/file    { path, content }
POST   /api/workspace/:projectId/create  { path, type:'file'|'folder' }
DELETE /api/workspace/:projectId/delete  { path }
PATCH  /api/workspace/:projectId/rename  { oldPath, newPath }
GET    /api/workspace/:projectId/export  streams a .zip (archiver)

Error shape: { error: { message, code? } }. Codes used by the client:
WORKSPACE_MISSING, FILE_MISSING, ALREADY_EXISTS, INVALID_PATH, INVALID_PROJECT_ID.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SOCKET EVENTS (Socket.IO)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
client -> server:  terminal:create { terminalId, projectId, cols?, rows? }
                   terminal:input  { terminalId, data }
                   terminal:resize { terminalId, cols, rows }
                   terminal:close  { terminalId }
server -> client:  terminal:ready  { terminalId, cwd, shell, pid }
                   terminal:output { terminalId, data }
                   terminal:exit   { terminalId, exitCode, signal }
                   terminal:error  { terminalId, message }
On socket disconnect the server kills every pty that socket owns. Production
(Replit/Linux) uses bash; Windows dev falls back to $COMSPEC (cmd.exe).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
CLIENT — FILES CREATED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
client/src/services/api.js              fetch wrapper + workspaceApi + ApiError
                                        (isWorkspaceMissingError helper)
client/src/services/socket.js           one shared socket.io-client connection
client/src/services/workspaceStorage.js remembers { projectId, name } in
                                        localStorage for session restore
client/src/features/terminalSlice.js    terminal tabs / active / open / minimized
                                        / panelHeight state
client/src/features/workspaceThunks.js  all workspace async thunks
client/src/utils/cloudTree.js           server-tree <-> Redux-tree mapping,
                                        path helpers, remapTreeIds
client/src/utils/messages.js            shared user-facing strings
client/src/hooks/useExportWorkspace.js  downloads the .zip via blob + <a download>
client/src/hooks/useSyncToLaptop.js     pulls tree + all files and writes them
                                        back into the imported folder handle
client/src/hooks/useRestoreWorkspace.js reopens the last project on reload
client/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.jsx  docked panel, tabs, +, resize,
                                                  minimize
client/src/components/Terminal/TerminalView.jsx   one xterm.js <-> one pty
client/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.css
client/src/components/Notices/StorageNotice.jsx         temp-storage banner
client/src/components/Notices/WorkspaceMissingNotice.jsx recovery banner
client/src/components/Notices/Notices.css

CLIENT — FILES MODIFIED
  features/fileSystemSlice.js  now models source 'demo' | 'cloud'; added
    setCloudWorkspace, refreshTree, remapPaths, markWorkspaceMissing,
    clearWorkspaceMissing, resetToDemo; refreshTree prunes dead tabs.
  features/editorSlice.js      added removeContent; resets on setCloudWorkspace,
    remaps buffers on remapPaths, prunes buffers for vanished files.
  features/uiSlice.js          isStorageNoticeDismissed + dismissStorageNotice.
  app/store.js                 registered the terminal reducer.
  App.jsx                      renders notices + TerminalPanel, resets terminals
    when the project changes, calls useRestoreWorkspace.
  components/Topbar/Topbar.jsx Download, FolderSync and TerminalSquare buttons;
    terminal + export + sync are disabled unless a cloud project is loaded.
  components/Topbar/Topbar.css workspace-chip.is-cloud + .topbar-divider.
  components/Sidebar/Sidebar.jsx  all new/rename/delete go through the workspace
    thunks when source is 'cloud'; demo behaviour kept for 'demo'.
  components/Editor/EditorArea.jsx  lazily loads cloud files via readWorkspaceFile.
  components/Editor/EmptyState.jsx  copy updated for the cloud workflow.
  hooks/useOpenFolder.js        picks a folder, reads ALL files, generates a
    crypto.randomUUID() projectId, POSTs /import, loads the returned tree.
  hooks/useSaveActiveFile.js    saves through saveWorkspaceFile (defaults to the
    cloud project); demo mode still refuses with an info toast.
  App.css                       .editor-column (editor + terminal stacked).

Client dependencies already present: @xterm/xterm, @xterm/addon-fit,
socket.io-client, lucide-react.

HOW THE PIECES CONNECT
  Topbar "Open Folder" -> openFolder() -> readAllFilesFromDirectory() ->
    importWorkspace thunk -> POST /import -> setCloudWorkspace(tree) ->
    saveWorkspaceRef().
  Explorer row click -> Redux only (open tab / expand).
  New/Rename/Delete -> createWorkspaceEntry / renameWorkspaceEntry /
    deleteWorkspaceEntry -> API -> refreshWorkspaceTree -> Redux.
  Editor open -> readWorkspaceFile -> setContent. Edit -> updateContent.
    Ctrl+S / Save -> saveWorkspaceFile -> markSaved.
  Topbar Download -> GET /export -> blob download.
  Topbar FolderSync -> GET /tree + GET /file for each path ->
    writeFilesToDirectory(original handle).
  Topbar TerminalSquare -> shows the docked panel. Each tab = one TerminalView =
    one xterm.js instance = one server pty.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
THE TWO IMPORTANT LIMITATION BEHAVIOURS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. TEMPORARY STORAGE:
   StorageNotice.jsx shows a dismissible amber banner ("This is temporary cloud
   storage. Export your project regularly to avoid losing work.") whenever a
   cloud project is open. Dismissal lives only in Redux, so it returns on reload
   — i.e. it is re-shown once per session, never permanently silenced.

2. LOST WORKSPACE (free-tier restart):
   The API answers 404 code WORKSPACE_MISSING whenever the folder is gone.
   api.js turns that into ApiError.isWorkspaceMissing, every thunk calls
   handleFailure() which dispatches markWorkspaceMissing(), and
   WorkspaceMissingNotice.jsx then shows:
     "This project's files were lost due to server restart (free tier
      limitation). Please re-import from your last exported backup."
   with a "Re-import project" button that resets to demo and reopens the import
   picker. This also covers the reload path: useRestoreWorkspace() reopens the
   saved project and a 404 there shows the same recovery banner.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
TERMINAL PANEL DETAILS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - Docked at the bottom of the editor column; height is draggable via the thin
    bar at its top, clamped to [100px, 70% of viewport].
  - Minimize collapses it to the 32px tab bar (ChevronDown/ChevronUp).
  - Individual tabs close with their X (emits terminal:close and kills the pty).
  - + opens a new tab (crypto.randomUUID id, title "bash N").
  - All TerminalView instances stay mounted (hidden via visibility), so switching
    or minimizing never kills a shell.
  - xterm theme matches #1e1e1e / #cccccc / cursor #ffffff.
  - The Topbar toggle is disabled (with an explanatory tooltip) unless a cloud
    project is loaded — there is no server process for laptop-only files.
  - FitAddon + a ResizeObserver keep the pty cols/rows in sync; term.onData
    emits terminal:input.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERIFICATION PERFORMED THIS ENTRY
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - oxlint: 0 warnings, 0 errors (36 files).
  - vite build: succeeds.
  - node --check on every server source file: passes.
  - Live API run (node server.js, no MongoDB): health ok; import wrote 2 files;
    tree, read, save, create, rename, delete all returned the expected JSON; a
    missing workspace returned 404; export returned Content-Type application/zip
    with an attachment filename and a valid zip body.
  - Live Socket.IO run: connect -> terminal:create ack { ok: true }, server
    emitted terminal:ready with cwd = workspaces/<projectId> and shell cmd.exe
    (Windows), sent `echo HELLO_PTY`, and terminal:output streamed the echoed
    text back -> OUTPUT_OK.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NOTES FOR THE NEXT DEVELOPER / AI TOOL
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - Do NOT store file contents in MongoDB. Metadata only, always.
  - Any new workspace route must run ensureWorkspace (or explicitly check
    workspaceExists) and must never trust a path — use the resolveInsideWorkspace
    guard in workspaceManager.js so traversal cannot escape the folder.
  - To run the UI you need BOTH processes: server on :4000 and vite on :5173.
    VITE_API_URL / VITE_SOCKET_URL override the API/socket base URL.
  - node-pty is an optionalDependency and is imported dynamically; if it fails
    to load the server still boots and terminal:create answers with a clear error
    (health reports terminal: "unavailable").
  - node_modules and .git are skipped during import (SKIP_DIRECTORIES in
    fileSystemAccess.js) — a normal JS project still imports fine.
  - The client still has the older handle-registry helpers in
    services/fileSystemAccess.js (create/rename/delete on a live laptop folder).
    They are no longer used by the editor (cloud thunks replaced them) but are
    kept because they are harmless and may return for a future sandbox mode.
  - Binary files are imported/exported as UTF-8 text; the workspace is text-only.


================================================================================
AI Code Editor — Phase 4: Authentication, Topbar Redesign, Resizable Sidebar,
               Activity Bar + Search in Files
================================================================================

STATUS: complete. Start the API (`cd server && node server.js`) and the client
        (`cd client && npm run dev`), then open http://localhost:5173 in a
        Chromium browser.

HEADS-UP: MongoDB is now REQUIRED for auth. server/.env already had a working
MONGODB_URI and register/login were exercised against it successfully. If
MONGODB_URI is empty, /api/auth/* answers 503 AUTH_UNAVAILABLE and nobody can
sign in, so all cloud features are unusable (by design — the toolbar gates them).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
IMPORTANT: AUTH IS NOW REQUIRED FOR ALL CLOUD/WORKSPACE FEATURES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Every route under /api/workspace is protected:
  protect (JWT)  ->  validateProjectId  ->  authorizeProject (ownership)  ->
  ensureWorkspace (disk exists)

On the client, Open/Import Folder, Export, Sync, Save and the Terminal toggle
only run for a signed-in user. If they are clicked while signed out, the sign-in
modal opens with "Please sign in to continue" (see useAuthGuard). The workspace
remembered in localStorage is only reopened once authenticated, and signing out
resets the editor to the demo workspace and clears the caches.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PART 1 — AUTH (BACKEND)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEW API ROUTES
  POST /api/auth/register  { name, email, password }
       -> 201 { token, user: { id, name, email } }
       errors: 400 INVALID_NAME/INVALID_EMAIL/INVALID_PASSWORD,
               409 EMAIL_TAKEN, 503 AUTH_UNAVAILABLE
  POST /api/auth/login     { email, password }
       -> 200 { token, user } ; 401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS (same message for a bad
       email and a bad password, so emails cannot be enumerated)
  GET  /api/auth/me        Authorization: Bearer <token>  -> { user }

NEW SERVER FILES
  server/src/models/User.model.js         name/email(unique, lowercase)/password;
                                          bcrypt pre-save hash + comparePassword()
  server/src/middlewares/authMiddleware.js  protect: verifies Bearer JWT,
                                          sets req.user = { id }
  server/src/controllers/auth.controller.js
  server/src/routes/auth.routes.js
  server/src/middlewares/authorizeProject.js  ownership check (see below)

MODIFIED SERVER FILES
  server/src/models/Project.model.js   ownerId (String) REPLACED by
                                        owner: ObjectId ref 'User' (required)
  server/src/models/project.repository.js  upsertProject({ ..., owner })
  server/src/routes/workspace.routes.js    protect + authorizeProject on all routes
  server/src/routes/index.js               mounts /api/auth
  server/src/controllers/workspace.controller.js  owner always from req.user.id
                                        (never from the body); max import 5000 files
  server/src/config/env.js             + jwtSecret (JWT_SECRET), jwtExpiresIn (7d)
  server/.env.example                  + JWT_SECRET + JWT_EXPIRES_IN (DEFAULT_OWNER_ID
                                        removed; it no longer exists)
  server/package.json                  + bcryptjs, jsonwebtoken

OWNERSHIP RULE (authorizeProject.js)
  project exists && owner !== req.user.id      -> 403 FORBIDDEN
  project exists && owner matches              -> continue (req.project attached)
  project not found                            -> continue, because POST .../import
     legitimately targets an id with no metadata yet; disk access is still guarded
     by ensureWorkspace (404 WORKSPACE_MISSING)
  no database connected                        -> check skipped (local/dev only)

JWT payload is { id: user._id }, signed with JWT_SECRET, expires in 7d
(JWT_EXPIRES_IN). If JWT_SECRET is unset the server falls back to a dev value —
production MUST set a real secret (see .env.example for a generator command).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
PART 2 — AUTH (FRONTEND) + NEW REDUX STATE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEW CLIENT FILES
  client/src/features/authSlice.js          state { user, token, isAuthenticated,
                                            loading, error }; thunks registerUser,
                                            loginUser, fetchCurrentUser, logout
  client/src/components/Auth/AuthModal.jsx  modal with Sign In / Sign Up tabs,
                                            inline validation, spinner, switch link
  client/src/components/Auth/AuthModal.css
  client/src/hooks/useAuthGuard.js          guard(run, message) -> runs when signed
                                            in, otherwise opens the sign-in modal

NEW REDUX STATE (auth)
  { user: null, token: <from localStorage>, isAuthenticated: <!!token>,
    loading: false, error: null }
  On login/register the token is stored under localStorage key
  'code-matrix:token' and every API call attaches
  `Authorization: Bearer <token>` automatically (services/api.js reads it per call).
  fetchCurrentUser() runs once on app load to validate/restore the session; only a
  401 clears the token, a network error does not.

NEW REDUX STATE (ui)
  activeSidebarView: 'explorer' | 'search'      (Activity Bar selection)
  sidebarWidth: number (default 250, clamp 180..500, persisted to localStorage
                under 'code-matrix:sidebarWidth')
  revealedNode: { id, nonce } | null            (scroll + flash a tree row)
  revealLine: { fileId, line, nonce } | null    (scroll Monaco + flash a line)
  authModal: { isOpen, mode: 'signin'|'signup', message }
  (+ the earlier toast / sidebar / AI-panel / storage-notice fields)

services/api.js additions: getAuthToken / setAuthToken / clearAuthToken and an
`authApi` object (register, login, me). `authApi`/workspace calls share the same
fetch wrapper, so auth is applied uniformly.

PART 3 — TOPBAR REDESIGN (three sections)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
LEFT   sidebar toggle, a reserved logo placeholder div (// TODO: logo will be
       added later) + "CodeMatrix" text, and the workspace chip.
CENTER the Quick Open search (see below).
RIGHT  Open Folder, Export, Sync, Save, Terminal, AI toggle, Settings, then a
       divider and the auth area (Sign In ghost + Sign Up primary buttons, or the
       user avatar with a dropdown containing Settings and Logout).

THE TWO DIFFERENT SEARCHES — DO NOT CONFUSE THEM
  1. TOPBAR QUICK OPEN (client/src/components/Topbar/TopbarSearch.jsx)
     - VS Code Ctrl+P style. Matches FILENAMES/FOLDERNAMES only, case-insensitive
       substring, any depth, max 8 results.
     - Keyboard: ArrowUp/Down to move, Enter to open, Escape to close; clicking
       outside closes it. Ctrl/Cmd+P focuses it from anywhere
       (see useGlobalShortcuts + TOPBAR_SEARCH_INPUT_ID).
     - Selecting a result opens the file in the editor (or expands a folder),
       switches the Activity Bar back to Explorer, expands ancestors and flashes
       the row (ui.revealedNode -> .tree-row.is-revealed, 1.5s fade).
  2. SEARCH IN FILES PANEL (client/src/components/Sidebar/SearchInFiles.jsx)
     - VS Code Ctrl+Shift+F style, lives in the sidebar (Activity Bar > Search).
     - Searches BOTH names AND FILE CONTENTS (case-insensitive substring).
     - Debounced 300ms; results grouped per file with a match-count badge and
       collapsible line list (line number + <mark>-highlighted match).
     - Summary line: "N results in M files"; "No results found" and an idle state.
     - Clicking a line opens the file AND dispatches ui.revealLine so Monaco
       revealLineInCenter()s and flashes that line for ~2s.
     - Fetches contents through services/fileContentCache.js (session cache keyed
       `projectId:path`, cleared when the project changes, refreshed on save) and
       prefers the live Redux buffer for files that are already open.
     - Guards: MAX_FILES_TO_SEARCH = 400, MAX_LINES_PER_FILE = 50.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. ALL DEMO / DUMMY DATA REMOVED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
client/src/data/dummyFileTree.js is DELETED (the now-empty src/data/ directory

    workspace = { name: null, source: 'none', projectId: null }, tree = [],

With no project imported the Explorer renders NO rows at all: just the "No
project imported — click Import Folder" notice in the sidebar header area and
nothing beneath it in the tree region.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. IMPORT FLOW — NOW REAL END TO END
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The top-bar button was renamed "Open Folder" -> "Import Folder" and now uses the
lucide FolderInput icon (it imports to cloud storage, it does not merely open a
local folder). Its behaviour in hooks/useOpenFolder.js is:
  1. isFileSystemAccessSupported() first; if false, a red toast with
     UNSUPPORTED_MESSAGE is shown and nothing else happens.
  2. window.showDirectoryPicker() via services/fileSystemAccess.js openFolder().
  3. readAllFilesFromDirectory() walks the folder recursively (skipping
     node_modules and .git) and returns { relativePath, content } records.
  4. POST /api/projects mints a crypto.randomUUID() projectId and a MongoDB
     Project document owned by req.user.id, and creates the disk folder.
  5. POST /api/workspace/:projectId/import writes every file server-side.
  6. dispatch(reopenWorkspace(...)) calls GET .../tree and replaces the Redux
     tree with the REAL backend tree, then the folder handle is stored for Sync.
A loading state shows "Importing project..." (spinner + disabled button), and a
success toast reports the file count. Failures show an error toast; a cancelled
picker (AbortError) is ignored. When the user is not signed in, the button
routes through useAuthGuard and opens the Sign In modal instead of importing.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. TERMINAL — IMPLEMENTED END TO END (NOT JUST UI)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Backend (verified working, not rewritten beyond comment removal):
  - node-pty@1.1.0 is installed; utils/terminalManager.js dynamically imports it
    and degrades to a TERMINAL_UNAVAILABLE 503 with a clear reason if it fails.
  - A pty is spawned per terminal with cwd = server/workspaces/<projectId> and
    the platform shell (COMSPEC/cmd.exe on Windows, $SHELL/bash elsewhere).
  - socket/terminalSocket.js registers terminal:create / terminal:input /
    terminal:resize / terminal:close, and kills every pty owned by a socket on
    disconnect. server.js also calls killAllTerminals() on SIGINT/SIGTERM.
Frontend:
  - components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.jsx is a VS Code-style docked bottom panel
    with a tab bar, a "+" for new tabs, a per-tab X (emits terminal:close and
    removes the tab), a drag handle on the top edge for resizing, and a
    chevron minimize/expand toggle. TerminalView.jsx owns one xterm.js instance
    (@xterm/xterm + @xterm/addon-fit) per tab and shares the single Socket.IO
    connection from services/socket.js.
  - The topbar TerminalSquare toggle is DISABLED (with tooltip "Import a project
    first") until workspace.source === 'cloud' && workspace.projectId exist.
  - Real execution was confirmed in the end-to-end test below: a pty spawned in
the project workspace and `echo <marker>` returned the marker, proving the
shell physically ran a command and streamed its output back over the socket.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. EXPORT + SYNC — NOW REAL END TO END
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - GET /api/workspace/:projectId/export streams a zip produced by archiver with
    Content-Type: application/zip and
    Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="<safe-name>.zip".
  - hooks/useExportWorkspace.js fetches the blob, creates an object URL, clicks a
temporary <a download>, then removes the anchor and revokes the URL — a genuine
browser download of project-name.zip.
  - hooks/useSyncToLaptop.js walks the server tree, re-reads every file over the
API, and writes it back into the originally selected local directory handle.
  - Both the Export and Sync topbar buttons, like Save and Terminal, are disabled
until a project is imported (tooltip "Import a project first").

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5. CODE STYLE RULE — NO COMMENTS IN .js / .jsx (EFFECTIVE FROM NOW ON)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Permanent rule for this project: do not add `//` or `/* */` comments to .js or
.jsx files unless a genuinely non-obvious workaround truly needs one. Prefer
clear names and small functions. Every .js/.jsx file touched by this pass had
its unnecessary comments stripped, and `grep -rn "//"` over both client/src and
server/src now returns only real URLs. CSS files are out of scope for the rule
and keep their section comments. Empty catch bodies use bare `catch {}` (or an
explicit early `return`) instead of a comment.

================================================================================
Phase 6: Login/Signup Connection Fix + Placeholders + Logo
================================================================================

STATUS: complete and verified end to end in a real browser.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. ROOT CAUSE OF "Could not reach the server at http://localhost:4000"
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The server was NOT down and the port was NOT wrong. server.js started and
listened on the PORT from server/.env (4000), health answered, and register
returned 201 + a JWT when called with curl. The failure was CORS:

  server/.env had:  CLIENT_ORIGIN=http://localhost:5173/   <- trailing slash
  browser sends:    Origin: http://localhost:5173          <- no slash

The `cors` package compares origins as exact strings, so the mismatch meant
Access-Control-Allow-Origin was NEVER emitted. The browser then blocked every
response from http://localhost:4000, `fetch` rejected with a TypeError, and
api.js's catch-all re-threw it as the misleading message "Could not reach the
server at http://localhost:4000. Is it running?" — which sent everyone chasing
a server that was running fine. (A CORS failure and a real network failure look
identical to fetch(); the message is inherently a guess.)

FIXES
  - server/.env: CLIENT_ORIGIN=http://localhost:5173 (slash removed).
  - server/src/config/env.js: resolveCorsOrigin() now strips trailing slashes
    from every configured origin, so this exact bug cannot recur.
  - server/.env: PORT= 4000 -> PORT=4000 (worked via Number(), but fragile).
  - Server restarted; verified with curl that Origin: http://localhost:5173 now
    gets Access-Control-Allow-Origin: http://localhost:5173 on simple requests,
    on the OPTIONS preflight, and on /api/auth/* responses.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH FOR THE API BASE URL
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - NEW client/.env and client/.env.example:
        VITE_API_BASE_URL=http://localhost:4000
  - client/src/services/api.js reads the base URL EXCLUSIVELY from
    import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL (legacy VITE_API_URL and the hardcoded
    localhost fallback are gone). If the var is missing it logs a console.error
    with setup instructions and throws ApiError code MISSING_API_BASE_URL
    instead of silently requesting a relative URL.
  - BUG FIXED: workspaceApi.exportProject() fetched a RELATIVE
    /api/workspace/:id/export path (it bypassed the base URL entirely), so
    exports would 404 against the Vite origin. It now prefixes API_BASE_URL
    like every other call.
  - client/src/services/socket.js: SOCKET_URL is now just API_BASE_URL — the
    legacy VITE_SOCKET_URL / VITE_API_URL lookups were removed. Socket.IO and
    HTTP share the same origin by design.
  - A grep across client/src confirms no other file constructs API URLs;
    auth, projects, workspace, export and the terminal socket all go through
    services/api.js.
  - NOTE: Vite reads client/.env at startup. The dev server here picked up the
    new file automatically; after editing client/.env, restart `npm run dev` if
    the value does not seem to take.
  - The summary's older note ("VITE_API_URL / VITE_SOCKET_URL override the
    API/socket base URL") is SUPERSEDED: only VITE_API_BASE_URL exists now.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. PLACEHOLDERS ON EVERY TEXT INPUT
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - AuthModal (shared Field component): Name "Enter your full name", Email
    "you@example.com", Password "Enter a strong password", Confirm password
    "Re-enter your password".
  - Topbar quick-open: "Search files and folders..." (kept).
  - Search-in-files panel: "Search" -> "Search across all files...".
  - FileTree: new file/folder inputs keep "File name…" / "Folder name…"; the
    RENAME input (previously placeholder-less) now has the same pair.
  - AIPanel composer: "Ask the AI assistant…" (kept).
  - Sweep confirms every <input> in client/src now has a placeholder; there are
    no textareas. (The terminal is xterm.js and exposes no text input.)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. LOGO (/logo.png from client/public) IN THREE PLACES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
NEW client/src/components/Logo/Logo.jsx — one shared component:
  - Renders <img src="/logo.png"> (root-relative, never an import, so it works
    in dev and in the production build; Vite copies public/ to dist/).
  - `size` prop = height in px, width keeps the aspect ratio.
  - onError -> console.warn(...) + a styled "CM" fallback badge (.logo-fallback
    in index.css, accent gradient), so a missing file never shows a broken-image
    icon. (The file exists — 1254x1254 — but the fallback is wired anyway.)

PLACEMENTS
  - Topbar left section: the empty "topbar-logo-placeholder" div (and its CSS)
    is gone, replaced by <Logo size={26}> immediately before "CodeMatrix".
  - Auth modal: the Lock icon was replaced by <Logo size={52}>, centred above
    the title (.auth-logo is now a centred flex wrapper).
  - Editor empty state ("No file selected"): the Sparkles icon was replaced by
    <Logo size={68}>, centred above the heading (.empty-state-logo likewise).

  - oxlint: 0 warnings, 0 errors (44 files).
  - vite build: succeeds; dist/assets/*.js contains http://localhost:4000
    (proof client/.env was read) and dist/logo.png is present.
  - node --check on server/src/config/env.js: passes.

    register/login/me in headless Chrome: ALL_PASS — register 201 + token,

    INVALID_CREDENTIALS, CORS headers visible to the browser.
  - Full UI drive of the real app over Chrome DevTools Protocol — 28/28 checks:
      topbar /logo.png renders (1254x1254), empty-state /logo.png renders,
      auth-modal /logo.png renders, all four auth placeholders correct,

================================================================================
Phase 7: Import Size Fix, EADDRINUSE Handling, Always-On Terminal, Favicon
================================================================================

STATUS: complete and verified. Terminal e2e: 26/26 checks in a real browser.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. "request entity too large" ON IMPORT — ROOT CAUSE + FIX
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ROOT CAUSE
  readAllFilesFromDirectory() uploaded EVERYTHING: only node_modules and .git
  were skipped, so a real project posted its whole node_modules tree (hundreds
  of MB) and express rejected the body with 413 "request entity too large".

FIXES
  - client/src/services/fileSystemAccess.js
      SKIP_DIRECTORIES = node_modules, .git, dist, build, .next, .cache
      (matched by folder NAME at every depth, never descended into).
      MAX_IMPORT_FILE_BYTES = 2MB — larger files are never read; their content
      is not fetched at all, so they never enter the payload.
      readAllFilesFromDirectory() now returns
        { files, skipped: { directories, oversizedFiles } }.
  - client/src/hooks/useOpenFolder.js — after a successful import, if anything
    was skipped, an info toast shows:
      "Skipped node_modules and other build folders — run npm install in the
       terminal to regenerate them"
    plus "Skipped N file(s) larger than 2MB" when applicable. The empty-import
    message now mentions build folders too.
  - server/src/app.js — express.json body limit: it was ALREADY 50mb (the spec
    suggested 25mb as a floor for headroom). It was deliberately KEPT at 50mb:
    lowering a size limit while fixing a size-limit bug would reintroduce 413s
    for 26–50mb payloads. With the client-side skips, real payloads are far
    below either number. (The limit lives in src/app.js, not server.js.)
  - Unit test (mock FileSystemDirectoryHandle): only src/a.js + small.txt were
    collected; all 7 skip-dirs (incl. a nested deep/dist) were never touched
    (their getFile() throws if descended into); the 2MB+ file was skipped
    without being read. ALL PASS. This path is unit-tested rather than
    browser-driven because showDirectoryPicker needs a native file dialog.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. EADDRINUSE PORT CONFLICT — ROOT CAUSE + FIX
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ROOT CAUSE
  server.js had no 'error' listener on the http.Server, so a second start (or
  an orphaned previous instance) crashed node with an unhandled EADDRINUSE
  stack trace, and `npm run dev` had nothing to free the port with.

FIXES
  - kill-port@2.0.1 installed as a devDependency in server.
  - server/package.json: "predev": "kill-port 4000 || true" — npm runs it
    automatically before `npm run dev`, freeing the port first. Tested twice:
    kills an active occupant AND exits 0 when the port is already free
    (`|| true` works fine under npm's shell on Windows and POSIX).
  - server/server.js: server.on('error') wraps listen —
      EADDRINUSE -> console.error("[server] Port 4000 is already in use. Stop
      the other process or the predev script will handle it next run.") +
      process.exit(1);
      any other error -> rethrown (crash with trace).
    Tested with a dummy listener on 4000: clean one-line message, exit code 1,
    no stack trace.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. TERMINAL — ROOT CAUSES, FIX, AND END-TO-END PROOF
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
ROOT CAUSES (why the terminal could never open)
  a) Topbar toggle had disabled={projectDisabled} — a dead button unless a
     project was imported.
  b) App.jsx render gate: showTerminal = isTerminalOpen && source === 'cloud'
     && projectId — even a dispatched toggle never rendered the panel.
  c) terminal:create required an existing workspace folder, so with no project
     the server answered 404 WORKSPACE_MISSING.
  d) (Fixed in Phase 6) the CLIENT_ORIGIN trailing-slash CORS bug also blocked
     the Socket.IO WEBSOCKET handshake (transports:['websocket'], no polling
     fallback), so the browser↔pty link could not be established at all.

FIXES
  - Topbar: the terminal toggle is always enabled and not auth/project-gated —
    onClick directly dispatches togglePanel().
  - App.jsx: showTerminal = isTerminalOpen (comment explains scratch mode).
  - SCRATCH WORKSPACES (the feature): TerminalPanel computes
      effectiveProjectId = workspace.projectId || `scratch-${userId}` ||
      'scratch-guest'
    and passes it to TerminalView. Server-side (terminalSocket.js): a scratch-*
    id -> ensureScratchWorkspace() (mkdir -p server/workspaces/scratch-<id>)
    before spawning; REAL projectIds keep the strict 404 behaviour. Once a
    project is imported its real projectId takes over for terminals (App.jsx
    also resets terminals when projectId changes).
  - workspaceManager.js: + isScratchProjectId / ensureScratchWorkspace
    (SCRATCH_ID_PATTERN = /^scratch-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,100}$/) — scratch ids are
    never registered in MongoDB; they are throwaway folders used as cwd only.
  - TerminalView.jsx: + window.__TERMINAL_VIEWS__ (Map terminalId -> xterm
    instance) — a debug/automation hook used by the e2e test to read the
    scrollback; entries are removed on unmount.
  - SECURITY NOTE: "always available" means the toggle is no longer behind
    sign-in, so anyone who can reach the server can open a shell in
    scratch-guest. Re-add the useAuthGuard() gate before any production deploy.

END-TO-END VERIFICATION (headless Chrome + CDP driving the real UI — 26/26)
  - toggle enabled with NO project -> click -> .terminal-panel docked at the
    bottom renders with the empty-state message.
  - "+" button -> tab with a live xterm instance; terminal:ready arrives over
    Socket.IO with cwd server/workspaces/scratch-guest.
  - TYPED "npm -v" into the xterm textarea -> echoed -> bare version line
    "11.16.0" rendered: real node-pty (cmd.exe) output, not a mock.
  - drag resize handle: 260px -> 340px; minimize chevron -> collapsed 36px;
    expand restores; both directions asserted.
  - sign up through the real modal -> terminal re-keys to scratch-<24-hex
    userId> and respawns there (asserted the exact id in the buffer).
  - second "+" tab -> two xterm instances; the tab's X closes only that tab;
    the remaining instance stays alive.
  - zero uncaught page errors, zero console.error.
  - TEST BUGS FOUND AND FIXED IN THE DRIVER (not the app):
      * a loose /\d+\.\d+\.\d+/ assertion matched the Windows banner
        "Version 10.0.26200" and false-passed before npm finished — now the
        test requires a BARE x.y.z line (and the command echo);
      * deriving windowsVirtualKeyCode from charCodeAt sent '-' as VK_INSERT
        (45) so "npm -v" arrived as "npm v" — the driver now dispatches proper
        DOM codes (Minus, KeyX, DigitN, Space).
