AI Code Editor — Terminal Session Persistence, a /health Cron Route, a Save-Work
               Help Topic, PWA Install Support, and the Agent Opened to All
               Users With the Vanilla Stack for Non-Admins
        (2026-10-11)
================================================================================

STATUS: complete for everything that can be exercised locally and VERIFIED by real
        runs (the server on :4000, and headless Chrome driven over CDP against both
        the production build and the dev app). NOT verified: the live Render and
        Vercel deployments, and a real external cron pinger.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
TASK 1 — TERMINAL SESSIONS NO LONGER DIE
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DIAGNOSIS (from the code plus the local server log; no Render log exists in this
checkout, so the Render-specific reading is reasoned from the code, not read)
  - The pty is NOT killed on a tab switch: the right sidebar keeps the Agent and
    Terminal views mounted, so switching tabs never unmounts the terminal.
  - The pty WAS killed on component unmount: TerminalView's cleanup emitted
    terminal:close. The docked panel's tab X and the sidebar's restart/shell change
    also close a session on purpose.
  - On a socket disconnect the previous server kept ptys for a 120s grace and then
    killed anything not reclaimed. There was NO server-side scrollback, so a
    reconnect to a live session replayed nothing, and a reconnect to a RESTARTED
    instance spawned a brand-new shell with an empty screen.
  - Socket.IO reconnects (autoConnect + reconnection true, websocket transport).
  - The observed "[process exited with code 0]" + a fresh prompt + a CHANGED
    HOSTNAME is the signature of the Render INSTANCE being replaced (a deploy or a
    free-tier restart): the old instance shut down, its shutdown killed every pty
    and emitted terminal:exit while the socket was still connected, the client then
    auto-reconnected to the new instance, which had no such session, so a new shell
    was spawned. That is the real cause. The cron ping avoids idle sleep but cannot
    prevent a deploy, a restart or an out-of-memory kill.

FIX (server — utils/terminalManager.js, socket/terminalSocket.js)
  - Grace period after a socket disconnect: 120s -> 30 minutes (SESSION_GRACE_MS).
    A pty is now closed only on an explicit close, on grace expiry, or on logout.
  - Per-session scrollback ring buffer (MAX_SCROLLBACK_LINES = 10000). On a
    (re)attach the server sends terminal:attach with the buffered output; the
    client resets its xterm and redraws it, so nothing duplicates and nothing that
    happened during the gap is missing.
  - Per-user limits (MAX_SESSIONS_PER_USER = 5). The userId comes from the JWT the
    client now sends with terminal:create; creating a 6th session closes that
    user's least-recently-active one first.
  - terminal:create reattaches an existing id; when the client says reattach:true
    and the session is gone, the server answers terminal:lost instead of silently
    spawning a second shell under the same id.
  - New terminal:ping / terminal:pong heartbeat handled server-side; a ping also
    refreshes the session's last-activity time.
FIX (client — TerminalView.jsx, SidebarTerminal.jsx, App.jsx, services/socket.js)
  - Unmount no longer sends terminal:close; an explicit tab X, a sidebar
    restart/shell change, and logout still do.
  - On mount and on reconnect the client reuses the SAME terminalId and asks to
    reattach, replaying the buffer.
  - A 22s heartbeat keeps the connection alive through the Render proxy; a small
    "Reconnecting..." pill shows while detached and disappears once attached.
  - A truly-lost session shows ONE line: "Session ended because the server
    restarted. Start a new one." (never a bare exit code).
NEW ROUTE
  - GET /health (app level, not under /api): 200 + "ok" as text/plain, no auth, no
    database call, no logging. POINT THE CRON JOB AT THIS URL.
  - The existing GET / and GET /health-check are unchanged.

FILES TOUCHED (Task 1)
  server/src/utils/terminalManager.js    scrollback buffer, 30min grace, per-user
                                         limit, session meta/scrollback getters
  server/src/socket/terminalSocket.js    token -> userId, reattach replay,
                                         terminal:lost, terminal:ping
  server/src/controllers/health.controller.js  getHealthPlain
  server/src/routes/status.routes.js     GET /health
  client/src/components/Terminal/TerminalView.jsx  detach-on-unmount, replay,
                                         heartbeat, reconnect pill, lost message
  client/src/components/Terminal/TerminalPanel.css  .terminal-reconnect
  client/src/components/RightPanel/SidebarTerminal.jsx  close the old pty on restart
  client/src/App.jsx                     close the user's sessions on logout
  client/src/services/socket.js          window.__SOCKET__ debug hook (automation)

VERIFICATION (real, against the local server on :4000)
  - Socket harness (client/tmp-terminal-verify.mjs, 7/7): a new session spawns, a
    command's output streams, ping/pong answers, a reattach after a real disconnect
    replays a scrollback that contains the earlier output, an unknown reattach
    answers SESSION_LOST, and a 6th session for one user closes exactly one session.
  - Real browser (tmp-harness/terminal-session-verify.mjs, 9/9, headless Chrome +
    CDP on the dev app): the sidebar shell runs `sleep 300`; switching
    Agent<->Terminal keeps the SAME pty and never emits an exit; a forced socket
    disconnect shows the Reconnecting pill, reconnecting reattaches the SAME pty
    with its scrollback intact, the pill disappears; killing the session and
    reconnecting shows the exact restart line.
  - GET /health answered 200, Content-Type text/plain, body "ok".

HONEST LIMITS (Task 1)
  - The cron ping keeps the free instance awake; it cannot stop a deploy, a manual
    restart, or an out-of-memory kill, and the free-tier disk still resets on a
    restart. A lost pty after any of those is expected behavior and now says so.
  - The Render/Vercel logs and hostnames were not reachable from this checkout, so
    the instance-replacement reading is inferred from the code + local log.

TASK 2 — HELP TOPIC "SAVE YOUR WORK"

    the same CommandBlock Copy buttons as the Freebuff topic): why the free-tier disk

TASK 3 — PWA (INSTALLABLE DESKTOP AND MOBILE APP)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - Manifest (client/public/manifest.webmanifest): name/short_name CodeMatrix,
    start_url "/", scope "/", display "standalone", background_color and theme_color
    #1e1e1e (the default theme), icons 192, 512 and a maskable 512.
  - Icons generated from the EXISTING logo (client/public/logo.png, 1254x1254 RGBA)
    into client/public/icons/ by client/scripts/generateIcons.mjs: icon-192,
    icon-512, icon-maskable-512 (logo on the theme background inside the safe zone),
    apple-touch-icon 180, favicon-16/32. TO REPLACE WITH THE FINAL LOGO: overwrite
    client/public/logo.png and rerun `node scripts/generateIcons.mjs` in client/.
  - index.html: manifest link, theme-color, apple-touch-icon, the favicon set, and
    the apple/mobile web-app meta tags.
  - Service worker (client/public/sw.js, registered from src/main.jsx via
    src/services/pwa.js): a versioned cache ("codematrix-static-v1") holding only
    the static shell (index.html, manifest, icons, logo, favicon) plus same-origin
    /assets and /icons at runtime. Navigations are network-first with the cached
    shell as the offline fallback. Everything dynamic is left alone (returned
    without respondWith), so /api/*, /socket.io/*, /preview/* and any upload or
    project list are network-only and can never be served stale.
  - Safe update flow: on activate the old caches are deleted; when a new worker
    installs while a controller exists the app shows a "New version available,
    reload" toast whose Reload button posts SKIP_WAITING and reloads on
    controllerchange.
  - Offline: the shell opens offline and client/src/components/Notices/OfflineNotice
    shows "The editor needs a connection. You are offline — reconnect to keep
    working."; nothing pretends to work.
  - "Install app" button in Settings > General (src/hooks/useInstallPrompt.js),
    shown ONLY when the browser fires beforeinstallprompt and hidden when already
    installed (display-mode: standalone).
  - Help topic "Install as app": Chrome/Edge steps (the install icon in the address
    bar) and the Safari iOS note (Share, Add to Home Screen).
  - client/vercel.json: the rewrite now keeps /sw.js, /manifest.webmanifest and
    /icons/* from being swallowed by the SPA catch-all, and headers set
    application/manifest+json for the manifest and application/javascript plus
    no-cache/no-store/must-revalidate for the service worker.
VERIFICATION: headless Chrome on the production build (tmp-harness/pwa-verify.mjs,
  13/13): Chrome parses the manifest with the right name/scope/display/icons,
  Page.getInstallabilityErrors returns no blocking errors, the theme-color matches,
  the SW controls the page, the cache is versioned, NO /api, /socket.io or /preview
  response is cached while the built shell is, the manifest and SW are served from
  the root, and the app shell plus the offline notice both work with the network
  offline. The update flow (tmp-harness/pwa-update-verify.mjs, 4/4) confirmed the
  reload toast appears, the v1 cache is deleted and v2 becomes active.

TASK 4 — THE AGENT IS OPEN TO ALL USERS; VANILLA ONLY FOR NON-ADMINS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  - The "admin only Agent" restriction is removed. Every signed-in account (role
    "user" or "admin") sees and can use the real AIPanel; the "Coming soon"
    placeholder is no longer rendered (its file is left on disk, unused). Signed-out
    visitors still get the sign-in prompt. The default right-panel tab is Agent.
  - Stack rule enforced on the SERVER (socket/agentSocket.js): a non-admin that
    names any stack other than "html" is rejected with a 403 and the message
    "Non-administrator accounts can only use the HTML/CSS/JS (Vanilla) stack."
    Because vanilla is the only allowed stack, a non-admin can never change the
    stack mid-task either.
  - Shell tool removed for non-admins (services/agentService.js): runAgent takes
    allowShell (from the verified role); the tool schema sent to the model is
    OPENAI_TOOLS_WITHOUT_SHELL when allowShell is false, and executeTool refuses
    run_command outright as a second layer. Admin behavior is unchanged.
  - The Vanilla pipeline does NOT depend on shell commands: compose_page,
    scaffold_site, scaffold_app, the quality checks and the vanilla gate are all
    server-side. A grep found runWorkspaceCommand used only by the run_command tool,
    so no server-side alternative was needed.
  - Settings restored for every signed-in user: the AI key section (Groq/Mistral
    keys, encrypted, password-gated reveal) and Strict Rules are visible and usable
    again, and App.jsx loads the masked key list for any signed-in account. The keys
    API route is now protect-only (ownership still enforced per user in the service
    layer), and Strict Rules still ride with that user's agent run.
  - Client stack dropdown (AIPanel.jsx): React, Next, Vue, Nuxt, Angular, Express,
    Nest are shown disabled with an "Admin only" tag for non-admins and cannot be
    selected; the default is HTML/CSS/JS (Vanilla); a stored non-vanilla preference
    is coerced to Vanilla for non-admins. Admins keep every stack.
  - The role field and /me role are unchanged; role changes stay manual in the DB.
    The terminal restriction is untouched. Per-user keys, usage and workspaces are
    unchanged and ownership checks are untouched.

  server/src/routes/apiKey.routes.js      protect only (all users)

VERIFICATION
  - server/tmp-agent-tool-verify.mjs (2/2): executeTool('run_command', ...,

================================================================================
================================================================================
Terminal: always the per-user container root, no remembered project, 60s grace
================================================================================

STATUS: complete. Everything below was verified locally (server on Windows,
production client build in headless Chrome); the live Render/Vercel deployment
was not exercised.

WHAT CHANGED

  SERVER
  - utils/terminalManager.js
    * SESSION_GRACE_MS: 30 minutes -> 60 SECONDS. After the last socket
      disconnect the pty stays alive for 60s (long enough for a page reload to
      reattach it), then the pty is killed and its scrollback buffer dropped
      with it. Explicit close, sign-out and the per-user cap are unchanged.
    * createTerminalSession(terminalId, socket, options) takes NO projectId any
      more. The cwd is ALWAYS the per-user container
      (ensureOwnerContainer(userId) -> workspaces/u-<owner>), and when that
      folder is missing it is created on the spot and the shell starts
      normally. The recreation is written to the SERVER LOG ONLY
      ("[terminal] recreated the missing container <path> for terminal <id>"),
      so the client can never show "[terminal error] This workspace is no
      longer on the server..." for this case. Only a genuine mkdir failure
      (permissions/full disk) fails that spawn, as TERMINAL_ROOT_FAILED, and it
      is logged to the server console too.
    * the session object no longer stores projectId — a terminal session now
      carries no project reference at all.
  - socket/terminalSocket.js: terminal:create ignores any projectId sent in the
    payload; the scratch-workspace branch and the "A valid projectId is
    required" branch are deleted, so there is no code path left that can start
    a terminal inside a remembered project folder.
  - utils/workspaceManager.js: getTerminalRootPath(projectId) removed — it was
    the "one level above the remembered project" path builder. Auth, token
    verification, ownership checks and every other workspace API are untouched.

  CLIENT
  - TerminalView.jsx, SplitTerminalView.jsx, TerminalPanel.jsx,
    SidebarTerminal.jsx: the projectId prop/selector, effectiveProjectId and
    the scratchProjectIdFor import are gone. Neither the sidebar terminal nor
    the floating/docked panel selects, sends or remembers a project or cwd.
  - NEW services/terminalSessionStorage.js: stores TERMINAL IDS ONLY (the
    sidebar id, the panel's groups/active ids/panel-open flag) in
    sessionStorage — never a project, cwd or folder reference. sessionStorage
    is per-tab and dies with the tab, so a closed tab never tries to reattach;
    a reload inside the grace window restores the ids and reattaches the SAME
    ptys with their scrollback. Records are also ignored when the auth session
    that reads them differs from the one that wrote them (a guest's ids are not
    reused after sign-in, or vice versa), so a signed-in user's terminal can
    never keep running in the guest container.
  - App.jsx: the signed-out teardown now closes/resets terminals only after a
    REAL sign-out (a ref remembers whether this page session was ever
    authenticated). It used to run on every page load, which would have wiped
    the restored ids on reload; before this change it was a no-op only because
    the state was empty. Its workspace/tree/cache cleanup is unchanged.
  - terminalSlice.js / sidebarTerminalSlice.js: read the stored ids at startup
    and write them on structural change (add/split/remove/shell/panel
    open+close); resetTerminals / resetSidebarTerminal clear them.

  LIFECYCLE NOW
    explicit close | sign-out | 60s after the last socket disconnect
      -> pty killed, buffer dropped.
    Agent <-> Terminal tab switches and panel minimize never drop the socket,
    so they still never end a session (unchanged).
    A terminal opened after the grace period is always fresh at the container
    root with no old output.

VERIFICATION (all local; the browser runs use the production build)
  - tmp-harness/terminal-root-verify.mjs — socket level, 19/19:
      container folder deleted -> terminal:create (payload still carries a
      projectId, proving it is ignored) is accepted, pty cwd is
      workspaces/u-termrootverify (container root, not a project/scratch
      folder), the folder is created, no terminal:error is emitted, and the
      recreation line is present in the server log; disconnect + 5s -> the same
      session reattaches with its scrollback replayed; after a real 66s wait the
      pty PID is gone (kill(pid, 0) -> ESRCH) and reattach answers
      SESSION_LOST; the next terminal starts at the container root with no old
      output; a second wipe + open works the same.
  - tmp-harness/terminal-root-ui-verify.mjs — headless Chrome, 34/34:
      sidebar terminal starts in workspaces/u-guest with no "[terminal error]";
      marker command runs; Agent -> Terminal -> Agent keeps the same session and
      process; Page.reload inside 60s reattaches the SAME terminalId with its
      scrollback; the container folder is deleted while the app is open and the
      next terminal starts at the recreated root with no error and no old
      output; the floating panel terminal also starts at the container root;
      after the window is closed the pty is still alive 3s later (grace), gone
      after 66s, and the server reports SESSION_LOST; reopening the window
      starts a fresh terminal at the root with no old output; then, as a
      signed-in user, the terminal starts in u-<userId>, a guest terminal id is
      NOT reused after sign-in, and a signed-in reload reattaches the same
      session with its scrollback.
  - regressions: client/tmp-terminal-verify.mjs 7/7 (spawn, output, ping/pong,
      reattach + replay, SESSION_LOST, per-user cap) and
      tmp-harness/sidebar-verify.mjs 36/36 (tab switch, scrollback round trip,
      split sessions, shell restart, resize/refit, reload, signed-in terminal in
      u-<userId> creating a folder the Explorer sees live, no page errors).
  - oxlint 0 warnings / 0 errors (101 files); vite build OK; node --check OK on
      every changed server file. No comments were added.

WHAT COULD NOT BE VERIFIED (honest list)
  - The live Render deployment: a real restart/deploy wiping the disk, and the
      60s timeout against a production socket. The 60s window itself WAS
      measured locally with real waits (66s) and a real PID liveness check.
  - The "shell - cwd" header line xterm prints on terminal:ready is wiped on
      this Windows/ConPTY dev setup by the shell's repaint, so the harness reads
      cwd from the terminal:ready / terminal:attach payload instead of from the
      buffer. That is pre-existing (not introduced here) and was not fixed.
  - Only one real signed-in account (created by the harness) was exercised; role
      differences are irrelevant to terminal start/cleanup, and no admin/non-admin
      pair was needed for this task.

STATUS SUMMARY (WHERE THINGS STAND)
  - Terminal start: ALWAYS the per-user container root (workspaces/u-<owner>),
    created on demand; no project, cwd or project reference is stored or reused
    for a terminal on the client or the server; a missing container is logged to
    the server console only and never shown as a terminal error.
  - Terminal cleanup: explicit close, sign-out, or 60 SECONDS after the last
    socket disconnect (replacing the earlier 30-minute grace), with the buffer
    dropped together with the pty. A reload inside 60s reattaches the same
    sessions (ids kept in sessionStorage, ids only); Agent/Terminal tab switches
    never end a session; a terminal opened after the grace period is always
    fresh at the container root.

    users with Vanilla-only for non-admins, the PWA, the "Save your work" Help
    topic, and GET /health.
